Appendix (Code Snippets, CLI, Make Targets)\n    23→\n    24→---\n    25→\n    26→## 1  Executive Summary\n    27→HoloCanvas is an open, **C-based** micro-service ecosystem that orchestrates the minting, governance, and market evolution of **generative, living NFTs**. The system exposes deterministic *creative recipes* on-chain while delegating heavy media processing to off-chain worker pods. Each component is decoupled via **Kafka** event-streams and **gRPC** contracts, allowing horizontal scale-out and plug-and-play experimentation with consensus algorithms, artistic strategies, and DeFi mechanics.\n    28→\n    29→---\n    30→\n    31→## 2  High-Level Blueprint\n    32→\n    33→```text\n    34→+------------------+        Kafka        +------------------+\n    35→|  Wallet-Proxy    | <---- Topics ----> |  Gallery-Gateway |\n    36→+--------+---------+                    +----+-------------+\n    37→         |  gRPC HTTP /1.1                   |\n    38→         |                                    |  Artifact REST\n    39→         v                                    v\n    40→ +--------+---------+       +-----------------+-----------------+\n    41→ |  LedgerCore      | <---> |   Mint-Factory (Factory Pattern)  |\n    42→ +--------+---------+       +-----------------+-----------------+\n    43→         |                                     ^\n    44→         |  RocksDB (UTXO + State)             |  Event\n    45→         v                                     |\n    46→ +--------+---------+       Kafka         +----+-------------+\n    47→ |  Governance-Hall | <---- Topics ---->  |  DeFi-Garden     |\n    48→ +------------------+                    +------------------+\n    49→```\n    50→\n    51→---\n    52→\n    53→## 3  Component Reference\n    54→\n    55→### 3.1 Wallet-Proxy (Proxy Pattern)\n    56→* Hides raw blockchain mechanics from UI clients.  \n    57→* Signs and bundles transactions, batching micro-payments into rollups.  \n    58→* Rate-limits requests per API key (Leaky-Bucket, per-tenant).  \n    59→\n    60→### 3.2 Gallery-Gateway  \n    61→* API layer (OpenAPI v3) that exposes queryable GraphQL interface.  \n    62→* HTTP → gRPC translation, static file CDN for media layers.  \n    63→\n    64→### 3.3 Mint-Factory (Factory Pattern)  \n    65→* Composes shader fragments, audio grains, and metadata into an `ArtifactRecipe`.  \n    66→* Emits `ArtifactCreated` events; persists recipe to **IPFS** and CID to chain.  \n    67→\n    68→### 3.4 LedgerCore  \n    69→* Custom UTxO‐style ledger written in C with **RocksDB** backing store.  \n    70→* Pluggable Consensus Engine (see §6).  \n    71→* Provides `LedgerService` (gRPC) with the following RPCs:  \n    72→  - `SubmitTx(Tx)`  \n    73→  - `StreamBlocks(Height)`  \n    74→  - `QueryState(Key)`  \n    75→\n    76→### 3.5 Governance-Hall  \n    77→* DAO voting, quorum tracking, parameter upgrades.  \n    78→* Runs embedded **SQLite** + **Raft** for internal persistence.  \n    79→\n    80→### 3.6 DeFi-Garden  \n    81→* Yield farming, staking pools, and fractional ownership of high-value NFTs.  \n    82→* Uses **libsecp256k1** for cryptographic ops.  \n    83→\n    84→### 3.7 Oracle-Bridge  \n    85→* Bridges external data (weather, stock, sensor) into on-chain events.  \n    86→* Provides `WeatherOracleService.Report(WeatherFrame)`  \n    87→\n    88→### 3.8 Muse Services (Observer Pattern)  \n    89→* Stateless pods that watch event streams (`ArtifactLiked`, `AuctionEnded`, etc.)  \n    90→* Trigger generative `Strategy` plug-ins that mutate media layers.\n    91→\n    92→---\n    93→\n    94→## 4  Event & Data Contracts\n    95→\n    96→### 4.1 Kafka Topics\n    97→| Topic | Publisher | Subscriber(s) | Payload Avro Schema |\n    98→|-------|-----------|---------------|---------------------|\n    99→| `tx.submitted` | Wallet-Proxy | LedgerCore | `TxEnvelope` |\n   100→| `artifact.created` | Mint-Factory | Gallery-Gateway, Muse-* | `ArtifactMeta` |\n   101→| `gov.vote.cast` | Wallet-Proxy | Governance-Hall | `VoteCast` |\n   102→| `defi.stake` | Wallet-Proxy | DeFi-Garden | `StakeEvent` |\n   103→| `oracle.weather` | Oracle-Bridge | Muse-Weather | `WeatherFrame` |\n   104→\n   105→### 4.2 gRPC / Protobuf\n   106→```proto\n   107→syntax = \"proto3\";\n   108→package holo.ledger.v1;\n   109→\n   110→message Tx {\n   111→  bytes  tx_id      = 1;\n   112→  bytes  sender     = 2;\n   113→  bytes  recipient  = 3;\n   114→  uint64 amount     = 4;\n   115→  bytes  signature  = 5;\n   116→}\n   117→\n   118→service LedgerService {\n   119→  rpc SubmitTx     (Tx)       returns (Ack);\n   120→  rpc StreamBlocks (Height)   returns (stream Block);\n   121→  rpc QueryState   (StateKey) returns (StateValue);\n   122→}\n   123→```\n   124→\n   125→---\n   126→\n   127→## 5  Life-Cycle State Machine\n   128→\n   129→```mermaid\n   130→stateDiagram-v2\n   131→    [*] --> Draft\n   132→    Draft --> Curated : curator.approve()\n   133→    Curated --> Auction : schedule.auction()\n   134→    Auction --> Fractionalized : reserve.price.met()\n   135→    Fractionalized --> Staked : defi.stake()\n   136→    Staked --> Archived : life.span.expired()\n   137→```\n   138→\n   139→States are enforced inside **LedgerCore** by a compiled **deterministic automaton** defined in `state_machine.c` (see Appendix).\n   140→\n   141→---\n   142→\n   143→## 6  Consensus Engine Plug-In Model\n   144→\n   145→`LedgerCore` dynamically loads shared-object libraries at runtime:\n   146→\n   147→```c\n   148→typedef struct {\n   149→    const char  *name;\n   150→    int  (*init)(void);\n   151→    int  (*validate_block)(const block_t *);\n   152→    int  (*commit)(const block_t *);\n   153→    void (*shutdown)(void);\n   154→} consensus_vtable_t;\n   155→```\n   156→\n   157→1. `libconsensus_pos.so` – traditional Proof-of-Stake.  \n   158→2. `libconsensus_da.so`  – Delegated-Artistry (weighted by curation reputation).  \n   159→\n   160→Each plug-in must export a `consensus_vtable` symbol.\n   161→\n   162→---\n   163→\n   164→## 7  Design Patterns In-Use\n   165→* **Factory** – `Mint-Factory` building artifacts.  \n   166→* **Observer** – Muse services listening to events.  \n   167→* **Strategy** – Generation strategies loaded by Muse pods.  \n   168→* **Proxy** – Wallet-Proxy shielding clients.  \n   169→* **State Machine** – Artifact life-cycle.  \n   170→* **Event Driven** – Kafka message bus.\n   171→\n   172→---\n   173→\n   174→## 8  Fault-Tolerance & Recovery\n   175→1. **At-Least-Once** event semantics; idempotent consumers use `dedupe_key`.  \n   176→2. **Circuit Breakers** guard cross-service gRPC calls.  \n   177→3. **Checksum** of every CID before pinning to IPFS & chain.  \n   178→4. **Snapshotting** Ledger state each 1000 blocks; restores via **RocksDB checkpoint API**.  \n   179→5. **Draining Mode**: Kubernetes preStop hook flushes pending tx to `tx.submitted`.\n   180→\n   181→---\n   182→\n   183→## 9  Deployment Topology\n   184→\n   185→```text\n   186→ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n   187→ │                  Kubernetes Cluster                    │\n   188→ │ ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐ │\n   189→ │ │ Wallet-Proxy  │  │ Gallery-Gate  │  │ Oracle-Bridge │ │\n   190→ │ └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘ │\n   191→ │        |^                 |^                |^          │\n   192→ │        v|                 v|                v|          │\n   193→ │  ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐│\n   194→ │  │ LedgerCore    │  │ Mint-Factory  │  │ Governance    ││\n   195→ │  └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘│\n   196→ │        |                                         |      │\n   197→ │        v                                         v      │\n   198→ │   ┌───────────────┐                        ┌──────────┐ │\n   199→ │   │ DeFi-Garden   │                        │   Kafka  │ │\n   200→ │   └───────────────┘                        └──────────┘ │\n   201→ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n   202→ ```\n   203→\n   204→*Observability*: Prometheus + Grafana dashboards for per-service latency & error-rate.\n   205→\n   206→---\n   207→\n   208→## 10  Appendix\n   209→\n   210→### 10.1 `state_machine.c` (excerpt)\n   211→\n   212→```c\n   213→#include \"state_machine.h\"\n   214→\n   215→static const char *state_names[] = {\n   216→    \"DRAFT\", \"CURATED\", \"AUCTION\", \"FRACTIONALIZED\",\n   217→    \"STAKED\", \"ARCHIVED\"\n   218→};\n   219→\n   220→static transition_t transitions[] = {\n   221→    { DRAFT,         CURATED,        EVT_CURATOR_APPROVE  },\n   222→    { CURATED,       AUCTION,        EVT_SCHEDULE_AUCTION },\n   223→    { AUCTION,       FRACTIONALIZED, EVT_RESERVE_MET      },\n   224→    { FRACTIONALIZED,STAKED,         EVT_DEFI_STAKE       },\n   225→    { STAKED,        ARCHIVED,       EVT_LIFE_EXPIRE      },\n   226→};\n   227→\n   228→artifact_state_t sm_next_state(artifact_state_t s, artifact_event_t e)\n   229→{\n   230→    for (size_t i = 0; i < ARRAY_LEN(transitions); ++i) {\n   231→        if (transitions[i].from == s && transitions[i].event == e)\n   232→            return transitions[i].to;\n   233→    }\n   234→    return s; /* No-op if invalid transition */\n   235→}\n   236→```\n   237→\n   238→### 10.2 Make Targets\n   239→\n   240→```make\n   241→build: ## Build all micro-services\n   242→\t@for svc in wallet_proxy gallery_gateway ledger_core mint_factory governance_hall defi_garden oracle_bridge ; do \\\n   243→\t\t$(MAKE) -C \"$$svc\" ; \\\n   244→\tdone\n   245→\n   246→docker-build: ## Build container images\n   247→\t@./scripts/docker-build-all.sh\n   248→```\n   249→\n   250→### 10.3 CLI Quick Start\n   251→\n   252→```bash\n   253→make build\n   254→docker-compose up -d kafka postgres\n   255→./bin/ledger_core --init-db\n   256→./bin/gallery_gateway --listen :8080\n   257→```\n Executive Summary  \n2. High-Level Blueprint  \n3. Component Reference  \n4. Event & Data Contracts  \n5. Life-Cycle State Machine  \n6. Consensus Engine Plug-In Model  \n7. Design Patterns In-Use  \n8. Fault-Tolerance & Recovery  \n9. Deployment Topology  \n10. Appendix (Code Snippets, CLI, Make Targets)\n\n---\n\n## 1  Executive Summary\nHoloCanvas is an open, **C-based** micro-service ecosystem that orchestrates the minting, governance, and market evolution of **generative, living NFTs**. The system exposes deterministic *creative recipes* on-chain while delegating heavy media processing to off-chain worker pods. Each component is decoupled via **Kafka** event-streams and **gRPC** contracts, allowing horizontal scale-out and plug-and-play experimentation with consensus algorithms, artistic strategies, and DeFi mechanics.\n\n---\n\n## 2  High-Level Blueprint\n\n```text\n+------------------+        Kafka        +------------------+\n|  Wallet-Proxy    | <---- Topics ----> |  Gallery-Gateway |\n+--------+---------+                    +----+-------------+\n         |  gRPC HTTP /1.1                   |\n         |                                    |  Artifact REST\n         v                                    v\n +--------+---------+       +-----------------+-----------------+\n |  LedgerCore      | <---> |   Mint-Factory (Factory Pattern)  |\n +--------+---------+       +-----------------+-----------------+\n         |                                     ^\n         |  RocksDB (UTXO + State)             |  Event\n         v                                     |\n +--------+---------+       Kafka         +----+-------------+\n |  Governance-Hall | <---- Topics ---->  |  DeFi-Garden     |\n +------------------+                    +------------------+\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3  Component Reference\n\n### 3.1 Wallet-Proxy (Proxy Pattern)\n* Hides raw blockchain mechanics from UI clients.  \n* Signs and bundles transactions, batching micro-payments into rollups.  \n* Rate-limits requests per API key (Leaky-Bucket, per-tenant).  \n\n### 3.2 Gallery-Gateway  \n* API layer (OpenAPI v3) that exposes queryable GraphQL interface.  \n* HTTP → gRPC translation, static file CDN for media layers.  \n\n### 3.3 Mint-Factory (Factory Pattern)  \n* Composes shader fragments, audio grains, and metadata into an `ArtifactRecipe`.  \n* Emits `ArtifactCreated` events; persists recipe to **IPFS** and CID to chain.  \n\n### 3.4 LedgerCore  \n* Custom UTxO‐style ledger written in C with **RocksDB** backing store.  \n* Pluggable Consensus Engine (see §6).  \n* Provides `LedgerService` (gRPC) with the following RPCs:  \n  - `SubmitTx(Tx)`  \n  - `StreamBlocks(Height)`  \n  - `QueryState(Key)`  \n\n### 3.5 Governance-Hall  \n* DAO voting, quorum tracking, parameter upgrades.  \n* Runs embedded **SQLite** + **Raft** for internal persistence.  \n\n### 3.6 DeFi-Garden  \n* Yield farming, staking pools, and fractional ownership of high-value NFTs.  \n* Uses **libsecp256k1** for cryptographic ops.  \n\n### 3.7 Oracle-Bridge  \n* Bridges external data (weather, stock, sensor) into on-chain events.  \n* Provides `WeatherOracleService.Report(WeatherFrame)`  \n\n### 3.8 Muse Services (Observer Pattern)  \n* Stateless pods that watch event streams (`ArtifactLiked`, `AuctionEnded`, etc.)  \n* Trigger generative `Strategy` plug-ins that mutate media layers.\n\n---\n\n## 4  Event & Data Contracts\n\n### 4.1 Kafka Topics\n| Topic | Publisher | Subscriber(s) | Payload Avro Schema |\n|-------|-----------|---------------|---------------------|\n| `tx.submitted` | Wallet-Proxy | LedgerCore | `TxEnvelope` |\n| `artifact.created` | Mint-Factory | Gallery-Gateway, Muse-* | `ArtifactMeta` |\n| `gov.vote.cast` | Wallet-Proxy | Governance-Hall | `VoteCast` |\n| `defi.stake` | Wallet-Proxy | DeFi-Garden | `StakeEvent` |\n| `oracle.weather` | Oracle-Bridge | Muse-Weather | `WeatherFrame` |\n\n### 4.2 gRPC / Protobuf\n```proto\nsyntax = \"proto3\";\npackage holo.ledger.v1;\n\nmessage Tx {\n  bytes  tx_id      = 1;\n  bytes  sender     = 2;\n  bytes  recipient  = 3;\n  uint64 amount     = 4;\n  bytes  signature  = 5;\n}\n\nservice LedgerService {\n  rpc SubmitTx     (Tx)       returns (Ack);\n  rpc StreamBlocks (Height)   returns (stream Block);\n  rpc QueryState   (StateKey) returns (StateValue);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5  Life-Cycle State Machine\n\n```mermaid\nstateDiagram-v2\n    [*] --> Draft\n    Draft --> Curated : curator.approve()\n    Curated --> Auction : schedule.auction()\n    Auction --> Fractionalized : reserve.price.met()\n    Fractionalized --> Staked : defi.stake()\n    Staked --> Archived : life.span.expired()\n```\n\nStates are enforced inside **LedgerCore** by a compiled **deterministic automaton** defined in `state_machine.c` (see Appendix).\n\n---\n\n## 6  Consensus Engine Plug-In Model\n\n`LedgerCore` dynamically loads shared-object libraries at runtime:\n\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    const char  *name;\n    int  (*init)(void);\n    int  (*validate_block)(const block_t *);\n    int  (*commit)(const block_t *);\n    void (*shutdown)(void);\n} consensus_vtable_t;\n```\n\n1. `libconsensus_pos.so` – traditional Proof-of-Stake.  \n2. `libconsensus_da.so`  – Delegated-Artistry (weighted by curation reputation).  \n\nEach plug-in must export a `consensus_vtable` symbol.\n\n---\n\n## 7  Design Patterns In-Use\n* **Factory** – `Mint-Factory` building artifacts.  \n* **Observer** – Muse services listening to events.  \n* **Strategy** – Generation strategies loaded by Muse pods.  \n* **Proxy** – Wallet-Proxy shielding clients.  \n* **State Machine** – Artifact life-cycle.  \n* **Event Driven** – Kafka message bus.\n\n---\n\n## 8  Fault-Tolerance & Recovery\n1. **At-Least-Once** event semantics; idempotent consumers use `dedupe_key`.  \n2. **Circuit Breakers** guard cross-service gRPC calls.  \n3. **Checksum** of every CID before pinning to IPFS & chain.  \n4. **Snapshotting** Ledger state each 1000 blocks; restores via **RocksDB checkpoint API**.  \n5. **Draining Mode**: Kubernetes preStop hook flushes pending tx to `tx.submitted`.\n\n---\n\n## 9  Deployment Topology\n\n```text\n ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n │                  Kubernetes Cluster                    │\n │ ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐ │\n │ │ Wallet-Proxy  │  │ Gallery-Gate  │  │ Oracle-Bridge │ │\n │ └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘ │\n │        |^                 |^                |^          │\n │        v|                 v|                v|          │\n │  ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐│\n │  │ LedgerCore    │  │ Mint-Factory  │  │ Governance    ││\n │  └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘│\n │        |                                         |      │\n │        v                                         v      │\n │   ┌───────────────┐                        ┌──────────┐ │\n │   │ DeFi-Garden   │                        │   Kafka  │ │\n │   └───────────────┘                        └──────────┘ │\n └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n ```\n\n*Observability*: Prometheus + Grafana dashboards for per-service latency & error-rate.\n\n---\n\n## 10  Appendix\n\n### 10.1 `state_machine.c` (excerpt)\n\n```c\n#include \"state_machine.h\"\n\nstatic const char *state_names[] = {\n    \"DRAFT\", \"CURATED\", \"AUCTION\", \"FRACTIONALIZED\",\n    \"STAKED\", \"ARCHIVED\"\n};\n\nstatic transition_t transitions[] = {\n    { DRAFT,         CURATED,        EVT_CURATOR_APPROVE  },\n    { CURATED,       AUCTION,        EVT_SCHEDULE_AUCTION },\n    { AUCTION,       FRACTIONALIZED, EVT_RESERVE_MET      },\n    { FRACTIONALIZED,STAKED,         EVT_DEFI_STAKE       },\n    { STAKED,        ARCHIVED,       EVT_LIFE_EXPIRE      },\n};\n\nartifact_state_t sm_next_state(artifact_state_t s, artifact_event_t e)\n{\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < ARRAY_LEN(transitions); ++i) {\n        if (transitions[i].from == s && transitions[i].event == e)\n            return transitions[i].to;\n    }\n    return s; /* No-op if invalid transition */\n}\n```\n\n### 10.2 Make Targets\n\n```make\nbuild: ## Build all micro-services\n\t@for svc in wallet_proxy gallery_gateway ledger_core mint_factory governance_hall defi_garden oracle_bridge ; do \\\n\t\t$(MAKE) -C \"$$svc\" ; \\\n\tdone\n\ndocker-build: ## Build 101→post_ctx_append(post_ctx_t *ctx, const char *data, size_t size)\n   102→{\n   103→    if (!ctx || !data || size == 0) return 0;\n   104→\n   105→    if (ctx->size + size + 1 > ctx->alloc) {\n In production deployments\n   286→ *   we may employ sharded, fine-grained locks or MVCC to improve throughput.\n   287→ */\n   288→static int tp_apply(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx, uint64_t memcpy(entry.tx_id, tx->tx_id, sizeof(entry.tx_id));\n   302→\n   303→    if ((rc = ledger_append(ctx->ledger, &entry)) != 0) {\n   304→        log_error(\"tp_apply: ledger_append failed (rc=%d)\", log_error(\"tp_apply: ledger_inc_nonce failed\");\n   311→        goto exit;\n   312→    }\n   313→    if ((rc = ledger_debit(ctx->ledger, tx->from,\n   314→                           tx->value + It is responsible for:\n *     1. Verifying basic and contextual validity of incoming transactions.\n *     2. Applying state-transitions to the on-chain ledger representation.\n *     3. Emitting domain events for downstream micro-services (Mint-Factory,\n *        Governance-Hall, etc.) via the event-bus.\n *     4. Surfacing metrics for observability (Prometheus/OpenTelemetry).\n *\n *   The code purposefully avoids any blockchain-consensus specifics; it only\n *   performs deterministic, replayable ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/*\n * tp_init()\n *   Allocate and initialise a Transaction Processor context.\n */\ntxp_ctx_t *tp_init(ledger_t Thread-safe.\n *\n *   Returns:\n *     0  – transaction accepted & applied\n *    >0 – transaction rejected (status code defined below)\n *    <0 – fatal/internal error\n */\nint tp_process(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len)\n{\n    if (!ctx || !raw || !raw_len) {\n        return -EINVAL;\n    }\n\n    /* Decode/deserialize */\n    tx_t tx;\n    memset(&tx, 0, sizeof(tx));\n    if (tx_decode(&tx, raw, raw_len) != 0) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_process: failed to decode transaction (len=%zu)\", raw_len);\n        pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n        ctx->stats.rejected_payload++;\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n        return 4; /* invalid payload */\n    }\n\n    const uint64_t ts_unix = (uint64_t)time(NULL);\n    const uint64_t t0_ns   = metrics_now_ns();\n\n    /* Validation pipeline */\n    if (!tp_verify_signature(&tx)) {\n        pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n        ctx->stats.rejected_sig++;\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n        return 1; /* bad signature */\n    }\n    if (!tp_verify_nonce(ctx, &tx)) {\n        pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n        ctx->stats.rejected_nonce++;\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n        return 2; /* nonce mismatch */\n    }\n    if (!tp_verify_funds(ctx, &tx)) {\n        pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n        ctx->stats.rejected_balance++;\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n        return 3; /* insufficient funds */\n    }\n\n    /* ── Critical section: apply & emit event ───────────────────────────── */\n    if (tp_apply(ctx, &tx, ts_unix) != 0) {\n        return -EIO; /* internal error applying */\n    }\n\n    tp_emit_event(ctx, &tx);\n    tp_record_metrics(ctx, \"accepted\", metrics_now_ns() - t0_ns);\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n    ctx->stats.accepted++;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n\n    return 0; /* success */\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Validation helpers                                                       */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/*\n * Signature verification\n */\nstatic bool tp_verify_signature(const tx_t *tx)\n{\n    if (!tx) return false;\n\n    /* Ed25519 public key & signature size are fixed (32 / 64 bytes) */\n    if (tx->sig_len != 64 || tx->pubkey_len != 32) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_verify_signature: malformed key/sig\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Hash the canonicalised, signature-free frame of the transaction */\n    uint8_t digest[32];\n    if (sha3_256(tx->frame, tx->frame_len, digest) != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_verify_signature: sha3_256 failed\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    const bool ok = ed25519_verify(tx->signature, digest, sizeof(digest),\n                                   tx->pubkey);\n    if (!ok) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_verify_signature: invalid signature for tx %s\", tx->tx_id);\n    }\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/*\n * Nonce verification\n *   Protects against replay and preserves causal order per-account.\n */\nstatic bool tp_verify_nonce(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx)\n{\n    account_state_t acct;\n    if (ledger_get_account_state(ctx->ledger, tx->from, &acct) != 0) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_verify_nonce: unknown account %s\", tx->from);\n        return false; /* account must exist */\n    }\n\n    const bool ok = (tx->nonce == acct.next_nonce);\n    if (!ok) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_verify_nonce: expected nonce=%\" PRIu64 \", got=%\" PRIu64,\n                  acct.next_nonce, tx->nonce);\n    }\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/*\n * Balance / funds verification\n */\nstatic bool tp_verify_funds(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx)\n{\n    account_state_t acct;\n    if (ledger_get_account_state(ctx->ledger, tx->from, &acct) != 0) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Gas cost: base + payload-dependent */\n    const uint64_t gas_cost = tx->gas_price * tx->gas_limit;\n    const uint64_t total    = gas_cost + tx->value;\n\n    const bool ok = (acct.balance >= total);\n    if (!ok) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_verify_funds: balance %\" PRIu64\n                  \" < required %\" PRIu64 \" (gas=%\" PRIu64 \", value=%\" PRIu64 \")\",\n                  acct.balance, total, gas_cost, tx->value);\n    }\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Application / state-mutation                                             */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/*\n * tp_apply()\n *   Mutate ledger state and any domain-level state-machines.\n *\n *   NOTE: called inside the global mutex for now.  In production deployments\n *   we may employ sharded, fine-grained locks or MVCC to improve throughput.\n */\nstatic int tp_apply(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx, uint64_t ts_unix)\n{\n    int rc = 0;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n\n    /* Append ledger entry – durable write-ahead log (WAL) */\n    ledger_entry_t entry = {\n        .timestamp  = ts_unix,\n        .tx_id      = {0},\n        .raw        = tx->raw,\n        .raw_len    = tx->raw_len\n    };\n    memcpy(entry.tx_id, tx->tx_id, sizeof(entry.tx_id));\n\n    if ((rc = ledger_append(ctx->ledger, &entry)) != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_apply: ledger_append failed (rc=%d)\", rc);\n        goto exit;\n    }\n\n    /* Update account nonce & balance */\n    if ((rc = ledger_inc_nonce(ctx->ledger, tx->from)) != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_apply: ledger_inc_nonce failed\");\n        goto exit;\n    }\n    if ((rc = ledger_debit(ctx->ledger, tx->from,\n                           tx->value + (tx->gas_price * tx->gas_limit))) != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_apply: ledger_debit failed\");\n        goto exit;\n    }\n    if (tx->value > 0 && tx->to[0] != '\\0') {\n        if ((rc = ledger_credit(ctx->ledger, tx->to, tx->value)) != 0) {\n            log_error(\"tp_apply: ledger_credit failed\");\n            goto exit;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* Domain-specific: NFT state transitions, governance votes, etc. */\n    switch (tx->type) {\n        case TX_MINT_NFT:\n            rc = artifact_state_on_mint(ctx->ledger, &tx->payload.mint);\n            break;\n        case TX_TRANSFER_NFT:\n            rc = artifact_state_on_transfer(ctx->ledger, &tx->payload.transfer);\n            break;\n        case TX_GOVERNANCE_VOTE:\n            rc = governance_apply_vote(ctx->ledger, &tx->payload.vote);\n            break;\n        case TX_STATE_MACHINE_EVOLUTION:\n            rc = artifact_state_transition(ctx->ledger, &tx->payload.evolve);\n            break;\n        default:\n            /* For payment-only transactions nothing else to do */\n            break;\n    }\n\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_apply: domain mutation failed (rc=%d)\", rc);\n        /* TODO: roll-back ledger_write via undo-log once implemented */\n    }\n\nexit:\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n    return rc;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Event-Bus integration                                                    */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic void tp_emit_event(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx)\n{\n    event_t ev = {\n        .topic = \"ledger.tx.applied\",\n        .ts_unix = (uint64_t)time(NULL)\n    };\n    snprintf(ev.key, sizeof(ev.key), \"%s\", tx->tx_id);\n\n    /* Copy raw transaction as payload; consumer services know how to decode */\n    ev.payload     = tx->raw;\n    ev.payload_len = tx->raw_len;\n\n    if (event_bus_publish(ctx->bus, &ev) != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_emit_event: failed to publish event for tx %s\", tx->tx_id);\n    }\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Metrics                                                                  */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic void tp_record_metrics(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const char *label, uint64_t dur_ns)\n{\n    if (ctx->m_metrics == METRICS_HANDLE_INVALID) return;\n\n    metrics_counter_inc(ctx->m_metrics, \"tx_total\", label, 1);\n    metrics_histogram_observe(ctx->m_metrics, \"tx_latency_ns\", label, dur_ns);\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Debug/Introspection helpers                                              */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nvoid tp_dump_stats(const txp_ctx_t *ctx, FILE *out)\n{\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    if (!out) out = stdout;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock((pthread_mutex_t *)&ctx->mtx); /* cast away const */\n\n    fprintf(out,\n        \"LedgerCore/TxProcessor stats:\\n\"\n        \"  accepted          : %\" PRIu64 \"\\n\"\n        \"  rejected_sig      : %\" PRIu64 \"\\n\"\n        \"  rejected_nonce    : %\" PRIu64 \"\\n\"\n        \"  rejected_balance  : %\" PRIu64 \"\\n\"\n        \"  rejected_payload  : %\" PRIu64 \"\\n\",\n        ctx->stats.accepted,\n        ctx->stats.rejected_sig,\n        ctx->stats.rejected_nonce,\n        ctx->stats.rejected_balance,\n        ctx->stats.rejected_payload);\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock((pthread_mutex_t *)&ctx->mtx);\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* End of file                                                              */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n","numLines":417,"startLine":1,"totalLines":417}}}

Once triggered, it takes a consistent snapshot of the mem-pool,\n *   builds a Merkle tree, computes the block header and forwards the block to\n *   the Consensus service via gRPC.\n *\n *   The implementation favours robustness: it features extensive error\n *   checking, clear separation between public and private API, and graceful\n *   shutdown semantics.  All heap allocations are guarded, and the worker\n *   thread is cancel-safe.\n *\n * Copyright (c) 2024, HoloCanvas Contributors\n * SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n */\n\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <inttypes.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n#include <stdatomic.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\n\n#include \"block_builder.h\"            /* Public interface for this module   */\n#include \"config/ledger_config.h\"     /* Constants, CLI / file overrides    */\n#include \"consensus/consensus.h\"      /* consensus_submit_block()           */\n#include \"crypto/crypto.h\"            /* sha256(), blake2b(), etc.          */\n#include \"mem_pool/tx_pool.h\"         /* tx_pool_*() API                    */\n#include \"utils/logger.h\"             /* log_debug(), log_error(), ...      */\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n * Static helpers forward declarations                                       *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic void *           builder_thread_main(void *arg);\nstatic ledger_err_t     build_block(struct block_builder *ctx,\n                                    struct ledger_block *out_block);\nstatic ledger_err_t     compute_merkle_root(const tx_list_t *txs,\n                                            hash256_t        out_root);\nstatic inline uint64_t  unix_epoch_ms(void);\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n * Public API                                                                *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nledger_err_t\nblock_builder_init(struct block_builder        *builder,\n                   const struct builder_cfg    *cfg,\n                   struct tx_pool              *pool,\n                   consensus_client_t          *consensus)\n{\n    if (!builder || !cfg || !pool || !consensus) {\n        return LEDGER_EINVAL;\n    }\n\n    memset(builder, 0, sizeof *builder);\n    builder->cfg       = *cfg; /* structure copy */\n    builder->pool      = pool;\n    builder->consensus = consensus;\n    atomic_init(&builder->running, false);\n\n    int rc = pthread_mutex_init(&builder->lock, NULL);\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        return LEDGER_EOS; /* Out of system resources (POSIX mutex) */\n    }\n\n    rc = pthread_cond_init(&builder->cond, NULL);\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        pthread_mutex_destroy(&builder->lock);\n        return LEDGER_EOS;\n    }\n\n    return LEDGER_OK;\n}\n\nledger_err_t\nblock_builder_start(struct block_builder *builder)\n{\n    if (!builder) return LEDGER_EINVAL;\n\n    bool expected = false;\n    if (!atomic_compare_exchange_strong(&builder->running, &expected, true)) {\n        /* already running */\n        return LEDGER_EALREADY;\n    }\n\n    int rc = pthread_create(&builder->worker, NULL, builder_thread_main, builder);\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        atomic_store(&builder->running, false);\n        log_error(\"Block-Builder: failed to spawn worker thread: %s\", strerror(rc));\n        return LEDGER_EOS;\n    }\n\n    pthread_detach(builder->worker);\n    return LEDGER_OK;\n}\n\nledger_err_t\nblock_builder_stop(struct block_builder *builder)\n{\n    if (!builder) return LEDGER_EINVAL;\n\n    bool expected = true;\n    if (!atomic_compare_exchange_strong(&builder->running, &expected, false)) {\n        return LEDGER_EALREADY; /* not running */\n    }\n\n    /* Wake the worker in case it's sleeping */\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&builder->lock);\n    pthread_cond_broadcast(&builder->cond);\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&builder->lock);\n\n    /* Worker thread is detached; best effort join semantics via poll */\n    const uint64_t deadline = unix_epoch_ms() + 5000;\n    while (unix_epoch_ms() < deadline) {\n        if (atomic_load(&builder->terminated)) {\n            break;\n        }\n        usleep(20000); /* 20 ms */\n    }\n\n    if (!atomic_load(&builder->terminated)) {\n        log_error(\"Block-Builder: timed out waiting for stop\");\n        return LEDGER_ETIMEDOUT;\n    }\n\n    pthread_cond_destroy(&builder->cond);\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&builder->lock);\n    return LEDGER_OK;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n * Worker thread                                                             *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic void *\nbuilder_thread_main(void *arg)\n{\n    struct block_builder *ctx = arg;\n    log_info(\"Block-Builder: worker thread started\");\n\n    int rc = pthread_setcancelstate(PTHREAD_CANCEL_ENABLE, NULL);\n    if (rc == 0) {\n        pthread_setcanceltype(PTHREAD_CANCEL_DEFERRED, NULL);\n    }\n\n    uint64_t last_build_ms = unix_epoch_ms();\n\n    while (atomic_load(&ctx->running)) {\n\n        /* Determine whether we should create a block */\n        bool should_build = false;\n\n        /* 1) Transaction count triggers */\n        size_t tx_count = tx_pool_size(ctx->pool);\n        if (tx_count >= ctx->cfg.max_txs_per_block &&\n            ctx->cfg.max_txs_per_block != 0) {\n            should_build = true;\n        }\n\n        /* 2) Time-based trigger */\n        const uint64_t now_ms        = unix_epoch_ms();\n        const uint64_t elapsed_ms    = now_ms - last_build_ms;\n        if (!should_build &&\n            ctx->cfg.max_block_interval_ms > 0 &&\n            elapsed_ms >= ctx->cfg.max_block_interval_ms) {\n            should_build = true;\n        }\n\n        /* 3) Manual flush (via condition) */\n        pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->lock);\n        if (!should_build) {\n            const struct timespec ts = {\n                .tv_sec  = (now_ms / 1000) + 1,\n                .tv_nsec = (now_ms % 1000) * 1e6,\n            };\n            pthread_cond_timedwait(&ctx->cond, &ctx->lock, &ts);\n        }\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->lock);\n\n        if (!atomic_load(&ctx->running)) break;\n\n        if (!should_build) continue;\n\n        struct ledger_block block;\n        ledger_err_t       err = build_block(ctx, &block);\n        if (err != LEDGER_OK) {\n            log_error(\"Block-Builder: failed to build block: %s\",\n                      ledger_err_to_string(err));\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        err = consensus_submit_block(ctx->consensus, &block);\n        if (err != LEDGER_OK) {\n            log_error(\"Block-Builder: consensus rejected block: %s\",\n                      ledger_err_to_string(err));\n            /* Implement your own policy here (retry, stash, ...). */\n        } else {\n            last_build_ms = now_ms;\n            log_info(\"Block-Builder: successfully proposed block %\" PRIu64,\n                     block.header.height);\n        }\n\n        ledger_block_free(&block); /* Free heap allocations in block */\n    }\n\n    log_info(\"Block-Builder: worker thread terminating\");\n    atomic_store(&ctx->terminated, true);\n    pthread_exit(NULL);\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n * Block assembly                                                            *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/* build_block()\n * Take a snapshot of tx-pool and compose a ledger_block ready for consensus.\n */\nstatic ledger_err_t\nbuild_block(struct block_builder *ctx, struct ledger_block *out_block)\n{\n    if (!ctx || !out_block) return LEDGER_EINVAL;\n\n    size_t snapshot_size = ctx->cfg.max_txs_per_block\n                               ? ctx->cfg.max_txs_per_block\n                               : TX_POOL_MAX_POP_BULK;\n\n    tx_list_t *txs = tx_pool_pop_bulk(ctx->pool, snapshot_size);\n    if (!txs) {\n        return LEDGER_EEMPTY; /* No pending transactions */\n    }\n\n    memset(out_block, 0, sizeof *out_block);\n\n    /* Fill block header */\n    out_block->header.version        = LEDGER_BLOCK_VERSION;\n    out_block->header.timestamp_ms   = unix_epoch_ms();\n    out_block->header.height         = consensus_next_height(ctx->consensus);\n    out_block->header.prev_blockhash = consensus_last_hash(ctx->consensus);\n\n    /* Merkle root */\n    ledger_err_t err = compute_merkle_root(txs, out_block->header.merkle_root);\n    if (err != LEDGER_OK) {\n        tx_list_free(txs);\n        return err;\n    }\n\n    /* Copy txs to block body */\n    out_block->txs      = txs;\n    out_block->tx_count = txs->count;\n\n    /* Finalise block hash */\n    crypto_hash256_ctx hctx;\n    crypto_hash256_init(&hctx);\n    crypto_hash256_update(&hctx, &out_block->header, sizeof out_block->header);\n    crypto_hash256_update(&hctx, out_block->header.merkle_root,\n                          sizeof out_block->header.merkle_root);\n    crypto_hash256_final(&hctx, out_block->block_hash);\n\n    return LEDGER_OK;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n * Merkle tree                                                               *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic ledger_err_t\ncompute_merkle_root(const tx_list_t *txs, hash256_t out_root)\n{\n    if (!txs || txs->count == 0) {\n        return LEDGER_EINVAL;\n    }\n\n    size_t leaf_count = txs->count;\n    size_t level_count = leaf_count;\n\n    /* Allocate working buffer – power of two rounding */\n    size_t capacity = 1;\n    while (capacity < leaf_count) capacity <<= 1;\n    hash256_t *buf = calloc(capacity, sizeof(hash256_t));\n    if (!buf) return LEDGER_ENOMEM;\n\n    /* Copy leaves (tx hashes) */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < leaf_count; ++i) {\n        memcpy(buf[i], txs->items[i]->tx_hash, sizeof(hash256_t));\n    }\n    /* Pad duplicate of last leaf if odd count */\n    for (size_t i = leaf_count; i < capacity; ++i) {\n        memcpy(buf[i], buf[leaf_count - 1], sizeof(hash256_t));\n    }\n\n    /* Iteratively hash pairs */\n    hash256_t tmp;\n    while (level_count > 1) {\n        size_t next_level = (level_count + 1) / 2;\n        for (size_t i = 0, j = 0; i < level_count; i += 2, ++j) {\n            crypto_hash256_ctx hctx;\n            crypto_hash256_init(&hctx);\n            crypto_hash256_update(&hctx, buf[i],   sizeof(hash256_t));\n            crypto_hash256_update(&hctx, buf[i+1], sizeof(hash256_t));\n            crypto_hash256_final(&hctx, tmp);\n            memcpy(buf[j], tmp, sizeof(hash256_t));\n        }\n        level_count = next_level;\n    }\n\n    memcpy(out_root, buf[0], sizeof(hash256_t));\n    free(buf);\n    return LEDGER_OK;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n * Utilities                                                                 *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic inline uint64_t\nunix_epoch_ms(void)\n{\n    struct timespec ts;\n    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n    return (uint64_t)ts.tv_sec * 1000ULL + (uint64_t)(ts.tv_nsec / 1e6);\n}","numLines":334,"startLine":1,"totalLines":334}}}

pthread_mutex_lock(&mgr->q_mutex);\n    if (mgr->q_tail) {\n        mgr->q_tail->next = node;\n        mgr->q_tail       = node;\n    } else {\n        mgr->q_head = mgr->q_tail = node;\n    }\n    pthread_cond_signal(&mgr->q_cond);\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&mgr->q_mutex);\n}\n\nstatic block_t *_dequeue_block(consensus_manager_t *mgr)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&mgr->q_mutex);\n    while (!mgr->q_head && !mgr->stop_requested) {\n        pthread_cond_wait(&mgr->q_cond, &mgr->q_mutex);\n    }\n\n    if (mgr->stop_requested) {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&mgr->q_mutex);\n        return node->blk;\n    free(node);\n    return blk;\n}\n\nstatic void *_worker_loop(void *arg)\n{\n    consensus_manager_t *mgr = arg;\n\n    for (;;)\n    {\n        block_t *blk = _dequeue_block(mgr);\n        if (!blk) break; /* Shutdown requested */\n\n        /* Step 1: Verify candidate block */\n        char *err_msg = NULL;\n        if (!mgr->engine.verify_block(blk, &err_msg)) {\n            logger_log(LOG_LEVEL_WARN,\n                       \"[ConsensusMgr] Block@%lu verification failed: %s\",\n                       blk->height,\n                       err_msg ? err_msg : \"(no details)\");\n            free(err_msg);\n            free(blk);\n            continue; /* Reject block */\n        }\n\n        /* Step 2: Finalise (exec state transitions) */\n        uint8_t new_state_root[32] = {0};\n        if (!mgr->engine.finalise_block(blk,\n                                        new_state_root,\n                                        &err_msg)) {\n            logger_log(LOG_LEVEL_ERR,\n                       \"[ConsensusMgr] Block@%lu finalisation failed: %s\",\n                       blk->height,\n                       err_msg ? err_msg : \"(no details)\");\n            free(err_msg);\n            free(blk);\n            continue; /* Inconsistent state, do not commit */\n        }\n\n        /* Step 3: Commit to durable storage */\n        if (!ledger_storage_put_block(mgr->storage, blk) ||\n            !ledger_storage_set_state_root(mgr->storage,\n                                           new_state_root)) {\n new_state_root[0], new_state_root[1]);\n\n        free(blk);\n    }\n\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Dynamic loading of Consensus Engine shared-library        */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic bool _load_engine(consensus_manager_t *mgr,\n                         const char          *so_path)\n{\n    mgr->engine_so_handle = dlopen(so_path, RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL);\n    if (!mgr->engine_so_handle) {\n        logger_log(LOG_LEVEL_ERR,\n                   \"[ConsensusMgr] dlopen (no pointers back into the SO needed) */\n    memcpy(&mgr->engine, sym, sizeof(*sym));\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic void _unload_engine(consensus_manager_t *mgr)\n{\n    if (mgr->engine_so_handle) if (!g_ctx.running)\n        return;\n\n    g_ctx.running = false;\n    pthread_join(g_ctx.thread, NULL);\n\n    rd_kafka_consumer_close(g_ctx.rk);\n    rd_kafka_destroy(g_ctx.rk);\n\n size_t len, event_t *out_evt)\n{\n    bool ok = false;\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loadb(json_str, len, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        log_debug(\"JSON parse error: %s (at line %d col %d)\", jerr.text, jerr.line, jerr.column);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* id */\n    const char *id = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"id\"));\n    if (!id || strlen(id) >= MAX_EVENT_ID_LEN) {\n        log_debug(\"Invalid or missing event id\");\n        goto cleanup;\n    }\n    strncpy(out_evt->id, id, MAX_EVENT_ID_LEN);\n\n    /* type */\n    const char *type_str = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"type\"));\n    if (!type_str) {\n        log_debug(\"Event.type missing\");\n        goto cleanup;\n    }\n\n    if      (strcmp(type_str, \"MINT_REQUEST\")     == 0) out_evt->type = EVT_MINT_REQUEST;\n    else if (strcmp(type_str, \"BID\")              == 0) out_evt->type = EVT_BID;\n    else if (strcmp(type_str, \"GOVERNANCE_VOTE\")  == 0) out_evt->type = json_object_get(root, \"ts\");\n    if (!json_is_integer(ts_node)) {\n        log_debug(\"Event.ts missing or not integer\");\n        goto cleanup;\n    }\n    out_evt->ts_epoch_ms = (uint64_t)json_integer_value(ts_node);\n\n    /* payload (keep raw to avoid extra serialise-deserialise cycles) */\n    json_t *payload_node = json_object_get(root, \"payload\");\n    if (!payload_node) {\n        log_debug(\"Event.payload missing\");\n        goto cleanup;\n    }\n    /* Dump just the payload subtree for downstream modules */\n    out_evt->raw_payload = json_dumps(payload_node, JSON_COMPACT | JSON_ENSURE_ASCII);\n    if (!out_evt->raw_payload) {\n        log_debug(\"Failed to serialise payload subtree\");\n        goto cleanup;\n    }\n\n    ok = true;\n\ncleanup:\n    json_decref(root);\n    return ok;\n}\n\nstatic void free_event(event_t *evt)\n{\n    if (evt->raw_payload)\n        free(evt->raw_payload);\n    memset(evt, 0, sizeof(*evt));\n}\n\n/*\n * dispatch_event – Call the appropriate domain handler.  Returns 0 on success.\n */\nstatic int dispatch_event(const event_t *evt)\n{\n    int rc = 0;\n    char tsbuf[ISO_TS_BUF_SZ];\n    iso_timestamp(evt->ts_epoch_ms, tsbuf, sizeof(tsbuf));\n\n    switch (evt->type) {\n    case EVT_MINT_REQUEST:\n        log_info(\"Dispatching MINT_REQUEST id=%s ts=%s\", evt->id, tsbuf);\n        rc = mint_engine_handle_request(evt->raw_payload);\n        break;\n\n    case EVT_BID:\n        log_info(\"Dispatching BID id=%s ts=%s\", evt->id, tsbuf);\n        rc = mint_engine_handle_bid(evt->raw_payload);\n        break;\n\n    case EVT_GOVERNANCE_VOTE:\n        log_info(\"Dispatching GOVERNANCE_VOTE id=%s ts=%s\", evt->id, tsbuf);\n        rc = mint_engine_handle_governance_vote(evt->raw_payload);\n        break;\n\n    case EVT_ORACLE_FEED:\n        log_info(\"Dispatching ORACLE_FEED id=%s ts=%s\", evt->id, tsbuf);\n        rc = mint_engine_handle_oracle_feed(evt->raw_payload);\n        break;\n\n    default:\n        log_warn(\"Unknown event type (%d) id=%s\", evt->type, evt->id);\n        rc = -EINVAL;\n        break;\n    }\n\n    return rc;\n}\n\n/*\n * iso_timestamp – Convert epoch_ms to ISO-8601 UTC string.\n */\nstatic char *iso_timestamp(uint64_t epoch_ms, char *buf, size_t buf_sz)\n{\n    time_t sec = (time_t)(epoch_ms / 1000ULL);\n    struct tm tm;\n    gmtime_r(&sec, &tm);\n    size_t n = strftime(buf, buf_sz, \"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S\", &tm);\n    snprintf(buf + n, buf_sz - n, \".%03\" PRIu64 \"Z\", epoch_ms % 1000ULL);\n    return buf;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── *\n * Signal Handling Convenience\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic void sig_handler(int sig)\n{\n    (void)sig;\n    log_info(\"Received termination signal, shutting down\");\n    evh_shutdown();\n    /* Exit entire process once clean-up completes */\n    _exit(EXIT_SUCCESS);\n}\n\n/*\n * evh_install_signal_handlers – Utility function that applications may call.\n */\nvoid evh_install_signal_handlers(void)\n{\n    struct sigaction sa = {\n        .sa_handler = sig_handler,\n        .sa_flags   = 0\n    };\n    sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);\n    sigaction(SIGINT,  &sa, NULL);\n    sigaction(SIGTERM, &sa, NULL);\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── *\n * Unit-Test Hooks (weak linkage, allow overrides)\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n__attribute__((weak))\nint mint_engine_handle_request(const char *payload_json) {\n    (void)payload_json; return 0;\n}\n\n__attribute__((weak))\nint mint_engine_handle_bid(const char *payload_json) {\n    (void)payload_json; return 0;\n}\n\n__attribute__((weak))\nint mint_engine_handle_governance_vote(const char *payload_json) {\n    (void)payload_json; return 0;\n}\n\n__attribute__((weak))\nint mint_engine_handle_oracle_feed(const char *payload_json) {\n    (void)payload_json; return 0;\n}\n\n/* End of file */","numLines":431,"startLine":1,"totalLines":431}}}

Thread safe.\n */\naf_status_e af_init(void)\n{\n    int rc;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n    if (g_ctx.initialised)\n    {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n        return AF_STATUS_OK; /* Idempotent */\n    }\n\n    /* Initialise external subsystems */\n    if ((rc = ledger_client_init()) != 0)\n    {\n        HC_LOG_ERROR(\"artifact_factory: ledger_client_init() failed: %d\", rc);\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n        return AF_STATUS_ERR_DEPENDENCY;\n    }\n\n    if ((rc = event_bus_init()) != 0)\n    {\n        HC_LOG_ERROR(\"artifact_factory: event_bus_init() failed: %d\", rc);\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n        return AF_STATUS_ERR_DEPENDENCY;\n    }\n\n    g_ctx.initialised = true;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n    HC_LOG_INFO(\"artifact_factory: initialised successfully\");\n    return AF_STATUS_OK;\n}\n\n/**\n * Tear down the Artifact Factory.\n */\nvoid af_shutdown(void)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.lock);\n\n    if (!g_ctx.initialised)\n    {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    ledger_client_shutdown();\n    event_bus_shutdown();\n\n    g_ctx.initialised = false;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.lock);\n    HC_LOG_INFO(\"artifact_factory: shutdown complete\");\n}\n\n/**\n * Mint a new generative artifact. - creator_addr  : Null-terminated creator wallet address\n *  - out_artifact_id (optional): Buffer receiving generated ID (hex-string)\n *\n * Returns:\n *  AF_STATUS_OK on success, otherwise error /* Nullable */)\n{\n    if (!g_ctx.initialised)\n    {\n        HC_LOG_ERROR(\"artifact_factory: must call af_init() first\");\n        return AF_STATUS_ERR_NOT_INIT;\n    }\n\n    if (!fragments ||     HC_LOG_WARN(\"artifact_factory: fragment validation failed\");\n        return AF_STATUS_ERR_VALIDATION;\n    }\n\n    /* Compose canonical JSON recipe */\n    cJSON *recipe_json = compose_recipe_json(fragments);\n    if (!recipe_json)\n    {\n        HC_LOG_ERROR(\"artifact_factory: failed to compose JSON recipe\");\n        return AF_STATUS_ERR_INTERNAL;\n    }\n\n    char return AF_STATUS_ERR_OOM;\n    }\n\n    /* Derive deterministic ID */\n    char artifact_id[ARTIFACT_ID_HEX_SIZE];\n    sha256_hex(recipe_str, strlen(recipe_str), artifact_id);\n\n    /* Persist    return AF_STATUS_ERR_DEPENDENCY;\n    }\n\n    /* Construct runtime artifact object (stack-allocated) for event emission */\n    artifact_t artifact = {\n        .state      = ART_STATE_DRAFT,\n        .created_at = time(NULL)\n    };\n    strncpy(artifact.id, artifact_id, sizeof(artifact.id));\n    strncpy(artifact.creator, creator_addr, sizeof(artifact.creator) - 1);\n */\n/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n\n/**\n * Basic heuristic validation of recipe fragments.\n * In production this would call out to a sandboxed compiler / linter.\n */\nstatic bool validate_fragments(const artifact_fragments_t *fr)\n{\n    if (!fr->shader || !fr->metadata)\n        return false; /* Mandatory fields */\n\n    /* Size checks (prevent DoS memory bombs) */\n    size_t shader_len   = strlen(fr->shader);\n    size_t audio_len    = fr->audio ? strlen(fr->audio) : 0;\n    size_t metadata_len = strlen(fr->metadata);\n\n    if (shader_len   > 64 * 1024   /* 64 kB */\n        || audio_len > 128 * 1024  /* 128 kB */\n        || metadata_len > 16 * 1024) /* 16 kB */\n    {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Metadata must */\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n/**\n * Compose the canonical recipe JSON object from individual fragments.\n * Caller must free returned cJSON with cJSON_Delete().\n */\nstatic cJSON *compose_recipe_json(const artifact_fragments_t *fr)\n{\n    cJSON *root = cJSON_CreateObject();\n    if (!root) return NULL;\n\n    cJSON_AddItemToObject(root, \"shader_fragment\",\n                          fr->shader ? cJSON_CreateString(fr->shader) : cJSON_CreateNull());\n    cJSON_AddItemToObject(root, \"audio_fragment\",\n                          fr->audio ? cJSON_CreateString(fr->audio) : cJSON_AddItemToObject(root, \"metadata_fragment\", meta);\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        /* Fallback as raw string to avoid data loss */\n        cJSON_AddItemToObject(root, \"metadata_fragment\",\n                              cJSON_CreateString(fr->metadata ? fr->metadata : \"\"));\n    }\n\n    return root;\n}\n\n/**\n * Compute SHA-256 digest and return as lowercase hex string.\n */\nstatic void sha256_hex(const char *input, size_t len, char out_hex[ARTIFACT_ID_HEX_SIZE])\n{\n    unsigned char hash[SHA256_DIGEST_LENGTH];\n    SHA256((const unsigned char *)input, len, hash);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < SHA256_DIGEST_LENGTH; ++i)\n        sprintf(out_hex + (i * 2), \"%02x\", hash[i]);\n\n    out_hex[ARTIFACT_ID_HEX_SIZE - 1] = '\\0';\n}\n\n/**\n * Persist artifact recipe into =========================================================================\n */\ntypedef enum {\n    QRY_OK = 0,\n    QRY_ERR_NETWORK      = -1,\n    QRY_ERR_JSON_PARSE   = -2,\n    QRY_ERR_NOT_FOUND                          qry_response_t *out);\n\n/*\n *  List every artifact token belonging to a gallery/collection.\n */\nqry_status_t gallery_query_list_artifacts(const char *gallery_id,\n                                          qry_response_t *out);\n\n/* =========================================================================\n *  Internal implementation details\n * =========================================================================\n */\n\ntypedef struct cache_entry_s {\n    char *key;              /* UTF-8 string key (token_id / gallery_id) */\n    char *value;            /* JSON blob                                */\n    size_t value_len;\n    time_t created_at;      /* For TTL eviction                         */\n\n    struct cache_entry_s *prev, *next;  /* intrusive LRU list          */\n    UT_hash_handle hh;                   /* uthash handle               */\n} cache_entry_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    size_t capacity;\n    size_t size;\n    cache_entry_t *entries;     /* hash table root               */\n    cache_entry_s *head;        /* Most-recently-used            */\n    cache_entry_s *tail;        /* Least-recently-used           */\n    pthread_mutex_t mtx;        /* protect all members           */\n} lru_cache_t;\n\n/* Global singleton state */\nstatic struct {\n    lru_cache_t   cache;\n    char helpers\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n */\nstatic int lru_cache_init(lru_cache_t *c, size_t capacity)\n{\n    if (!c || capacity == 0)\n        return -1;\n    c->capacity = capacity;\n    c->size     = 0;\n    c->entries  = NULL;\n    c->head     = c->tail = NULL;\n    if (pthread_mutex_init(&c->mtx, NULL) != 0) {\n        LOG_ERR(\"pthread_mutex_init failed: %s\", strerror(errno));\n        return -1;\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n\nstatic void lru_cache_evict(lru_cache_t *c)\n{\n    /* Remove oldest (tail) entry if above capacity or TTL expired */\n    cache_entry_t *entry = c->tail;\n    time_t now = time(NULL);\n\n    while (entry &&\n          (c->size > c->capacity ||\n           (now - entry->created_at) > DEFAULT_CACHE_TTL_SEC)) {\n\n        /* Detach from linked list */\n        if (entry->prev)\n            entry->prev->next = NULL;\n        c->tail = entry->prev;\n        if (c->head == entry)\n            c->head = NULL;\n\n        /* Remove from hash table */\n        HASH_DEL(c->entries, entry);\n\n        /* Free entry */\n        free(entry->key);\n        free(entry->value);\n        cache_entry_t *old = entry;\n        entry = entry->prev;\n        free(old);\n        c->size--;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic void lru_cache_promote(lru_cache_t *c, cache_entry_t *entry)\n{\n    /* Move entry to head of LRU list */\n    if (entry == c->head)\n        return; /* already MRU */\n\n    /* Detach */\n    if (entry->prev)\n        entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n    if (entry->next)\n        entry->next->prev = entry->prev;\n    if (c->tail == entry)\n        c->tail = entry->prev;\n\n    /* Insert at head */\n    entry->prev = NULL;\n    entry->next = c->head;\n    if (c->head)\n        c->head->prev = entry;\n    c->head = entry;\n    if (c->tail == NULL)\n        c->tail = entry;\n}\n\nstatic char *lru_cache_get(lru_cache_t *c,\n                           const char *key,\n                           size_t *value_len_out)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&c->mtx);\n    cache_entry_t *entry = NULL;\n    HASH_FIND_STR(c->entries, key, entry);\n\n    if (!entry) {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&c->mtx);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Refresh LRU position and check TTL */\n    time_t now = time(NULL);\n    if ((now - entry->created_at) > DEFAULT_CACHE_TTL_SEC) {\n        /* Stale – treat as miss */\n        LOG_INFO(\"Cache expired for key=%s\", key);\n        HASH_DEL(c->entries, entry);\n\n        if (entry->prev)\n            entry->prev->next = entry->next;\n        if (entry->next)\n            entry->next->prev = entry->prev;\n        if (c->head == entry)\n            c->head = entry->next;\n        if (c->tail == entry)\n            c->tail = entry->prev;\n\n        free(entry->key);\n        free(entry->value);\n        free(entry);\n        c->size--;\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&c->mtx);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    lru_cache_promote(c, entry);\n\n    char *dup = strndup(entry->value, entry->value_len);\n    if (value_len_out)\n        *value_len_out = entry->value_len;\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&c->mtx);\n    return dup;\n}\n\nstatic void lru_cache_put(lru_cache_t *c,\n                          const char *key,\n                          const char *value,\n                          size_t value_len)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&c->mtx);\n\n    cache_entry_t *entry = NULL;\n    HASH_FIND_STR(c->entries, key, entry);\n    if (entry) {\n        /* Update existing */\n        free(entry->value);\n        entry->value = strndup(value, value_len);\n        entry->value_len = value_len;\n        entry->created_at = time(NULL);\n        lru_cache_promote(c, entry);\n    } else {\n        /* New entry */\n        entry = calloc(1, sizeof(*entry));\n        entry->key   = strdup(key);\n        entry->value = strndup(value, value_len);\n        entry->value_len = value_len;\n        entry->created_at = time(NULL);\n\n        /* Insert into hash & LRU list */\n        HASH_ADD_KEYPTR(hh, c->entries, entry->key, strlen(entry->key), entry);\n        entry->prev = NULL;\n        entry->next = c->head;\n        if (c->head)\n            c->head->prev = entry;\n        c->head = entry;\n        if (c->tail == NULL)\n            c->tail = entry;\n\n        c->size++;\n    }\n\n    lru_cache_evict(c); /* Ensure capacity */\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&c->mtx);\n}\n\nstatic void lru_cache_deinit(lru_cache_t *c)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&c->mtx);\n    cache_entry_t *cur, *tmp;\n    HASH_ITER(hh, c->entries, cur, tmp) {\n        HASH_DEL(c->entries, cur);\n        free(cur->key);\n        free(cur->value);\n        free(cur);\n    }\n    c->head = c->tail = NULL;\n    c->size = c->capacity = 0;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&c->mtx);\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&c->mtx);\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  CURL helpers\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    char  *mem;\n    size_t size;\n} curl_resp_buf_t;\n\nstatic size_t curl_write_cb(void *contents, size_t size, size_t nmemb,\n                            void *userp)\n{\n    size_t realsize = size * nmemb;\n    curl_resp_buf_t *buf = (curl_resp_buf_t *)userp;\n\n    char *ptr = realloc(buf->mem, buf->size + realsize + 1);\n    if (!ptr) {\n        LOG_ERR(\"Not enough memory (realloc returned NULL)\");\n        return 0; /* abort */\n    }\n    buf->mem = ptr;\n    memcpy(&(buf->mem[buf->size]), contents, realsize);\n    buf->size += realsize;\n    buf->mem[buf->size] = 0;\n    return realsize;\n}\n\nstatic qry_status_t http_get_json(const char *url,\n                                  char **out_payload,\n                                  size_t *out_len,\n                                  long *out_http_code)\n{\n    CURL *curl = curl_easy_init();\n    if (!curl) {\n        LOG_ERR(\"curl_easy_init failed\");\n        return QRY_ERR_INTERNAL;\n    }\n\n    curl_resp_buf_t chunk = { .mem = malloc(1), .size = 0 };\n    struct curl_slist *hdrs = NULL;\n\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_URL, url);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_HTTPGET, 1L);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_FOLLOWLOCATION, 1L);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION, curl_write_cb);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEDATA, (void *)&chunk);\n    /* Accept JSON */\n    hdrs = curl_slist_append(hdrs, \"Accept: application/json\");\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, hdrs);\n\n    CURLcode res = curl_easy_perform(curl);\n    if (res != CURLE_OK) {\n        LOG_ERR(\"curl_easy_perform() failed: %s\", curl_easy_strerror(res));\n        curl_slist_free_all(hdrs);\n        curl_easy_cleanup(curl);\n        free(chunk.mem);\n        return QRY_ERR_NETWORK;\n    }\n\n    long http_code = 0;\n    curl_easy_getinfo(curl, CURLINFO_RESPONSE_CODE, &http_code);\n    if (out_http_code)\n        *out_http_code = http_code;\n\n    *out_payload = chunk.mem;\n    *out_len     = chunk.size;\n\n    curl_slist_free_all(hdrs);\n    curl_easy_cleanup(curl);\n    return QRY_OK;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Private utility helpers\n * -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n */\nstatic void populate_endpoints(void)\n{\n    const char *env;\n\n    env = getenv(ENV_LEDGERCORE_ENDPOINT);\n    snprintf(g_srv.ledgercore_ep, sizeof(g_srv.ledgercore_ep), \"%s\",\n             env ? env : DEFAULT_LEDGERCORE_ENDPOINT);\n\n    env = getenv(ENV_FACTORY_ENDPOINT);\n    snprintf(g_srv.factory_ep, sizeof(g_srv.factory_ep), \"%s\",\n             env ? env : DEFAULT_FACTORY_ENDPOINT);\n\n    env = getenv(ENV_GOVERNANCE_ENDPOINT);\n    snprintf(g_srv.governance_ep, sizeof(g_srv.governance_ep), \"%s\",\n             env ? env : DEFAULT_GOVERNANCE_ENDPOINT);\n\n    LOG_INFO(\"LedgerCore endpoint: %s\",      g_srv.ledgercore_ep);\n    LOG_INFO(\"Mint-Factory endpoint: %s\",    g_srv.factory_ep);\n    LOG_INFO(\"Governance-Hall endpoint: %s\", g_srv.governance_ep);\n}\n\n/* =========================================================================\n *  Public API Implementation\n * =========================================================================\n */\nint gallery_query_init(void)\n{\n    memset(&g_srv, 0, sizeof(g_srv));\n\n    if *  HoloCanvas -- Kafka Client Abstraction Layer\n *\n *  File:    HoloCanvas/shared/kafka_client/hc_kafka.c\n *  Project: HoloCanvas – A Micro-Gallery Blockchain for Generative Artifacts\n *\n *  Description:\n *      Thin(ish) wrapper around librdkafka that provides the HoloCanvas\n *      micro-services with a sane, opinionated interface for publishing and\n *      consuming protobuf / JSON events on the project-wide Event Bus.\n *\n *  Design notes:\n *      • One producer instance and one consumer instance are multiplexed out of\n *        a single rd_kafka_t handle to conserve file descriptors.\n *      • Thread-safety is provided by an internal mutex guarding the producer’s\n *        shared queue; the consumer runs in the caller’s thread.\n *      • Delivery reports are routed to a ring-buffer so that infrequent callers\n *        can inspect them without registering a full callback.\n *      • All public API symbols are prefixed with “hc_kafka_”.\n *\n *  Build:\n *      gcc -Wall -Wextra -pedantic -std=c11 \\\n *          -I/path/to/librdkafka/include -L/path/to/librdkafka/lib \\\n *          -lrdkafka -pthread -o hc_kafka.o -c hc_kafka.c\n */\n\n#include                         Compile-time Defaults                           */\n\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_PREFIX\n#   define HC_KAFKA_LOG_PREFIX  \"[hc_kafka] \"\n#endif\n\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_MAX_DELIVERY_RING\n#   define HC_KAFKA_MAX_DELIVERY_RING  1024  /* Power-of-two strongly advised */\n#endif\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                                 Logging                                   */\n\nstatic void hc_kafka_vlog(enum hc_kafka_log_level lvl,\n                          const char *fmt, va_list ap)\n{\n    const char *pfx = HC_KAFKA_LOG_PREFIX;\n    FILE *stream   = (lvl >= HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout;\n\n    flockfile(stream);\n    fprintf(stream, \"%s\", pfx);\n    vfprintf(stream, fmt, ap);\n    fprintf(stream, \"\\n\");\n    funlockfile(stream);\n}\n\nstatic void hc_kafka_log(enum hc_kafka_log_level lvl, const char *fmt, ...)\n{\n    va_list ap;\n    va_start(ap, fmt);\n    hc_kafka_vlog(lvl, fmt, ap);\n    va_end(ap);\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                        Ring-buffer for delivery reports                    */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    hc_kafka_delivery_t buf[HC_KAFKA_MAX_DELIVERY_RING];\n    size_t              head;\n    size_t              tail;\n    pthread_mutex_t     mutex;\n} delivery_ring_t;\n\nstatic void ring_init(delivery_ring_t *r)\n{\n    memset(r, 0, sizeof(*r));\n    pthread_mutex_init(&r->mutex, NULL);\n}\n\nstatic void ring_push(delivery_ring_t *r, const hc_kafka_delivery_t *item)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&r->mutex);\n    r->buf[r->head] = *item;\n    r->head = (r->head + 1) % HC_KAFKA_MAX_DELIVERY_RING;\n    if (r->head == r->tail) {          /* overwrite oldest */\n        r->tail = (r->tail + 1) % HC_KAFKA_MAX_DELIVERY_RING;\n    }\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&r->mutex);\n}\n\nstatic int ring_pop(delivery_ring_t *r, hc_kafka_delivery_t *out)\n{\n    int found = 0;\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&r->mutex);\n    if (r->tail != r->head) {\n        *out = r->buf[r->tail];\n        r->tail = (r->tail + 1) % HC_KAFKA_MAX_DELIVERY_RING;\n        found = 1;\n    }\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&r->mutex);\n    return found;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                    Internal structure representing a client               */\n\nstruct hc_kafka_client {\n    rd_kafka_t       *rk;             /* Unified handle (producer & consumer) */\n    rd_kafka_conf_t  *conf;           /* Base configuration object           */\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_t *subscribed;\n    pthread_mutex_t   producer_mutex; /* Guards produce() and queue length   */\n    delivery_ring_t   d_ring;         /* Ring-buffer for delivery reports    */\n    char             *brokers;\n    char             *client_id;\n    volatile int      run;\n};\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                        librdkafka Callback Shims                           */\n\nstatic void rebalance_cb(rd_kafka_t *rk,\n                         rd_kafka_resp_err_t err,\n                         rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_t *partitions,\n                         void *opaque)\n{\n    hc_kafka_client_t *cli = (hc_kafka_client_t *)opaque;\n    switch (err) {\n        case RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__ASSIGN_PARTITIONS:\n            hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_INFO,\n                         \"Assigned %d partition(s) to consumer\",\n                         partitions->cnt);\n            rd_kafka_assign(rk, partitions);\n            break;\n        case RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__REVOKE_PARTITIONS:\n            hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_INFO,\n                         \"Partitions revoked\");\n            rd_kafka_assign(rk, NULL);\n            break;\n        default:\n            rd_kafka_assign(rk, NULL);\n            break;\n    }\n    (void)cli;\n}\n\nstatic void logger_cb(const rd_kafka_t *rk, int level,\n                      const char *fac, const char *buf)\n{\n    (void)rk; (void)fac;\n    enum hc_kafka_log_level lvl;\n\n    /* Map librdkafka logs to our own */\n    if (level >= 6)\n        lvl = HC_KAFKA_LOG_DEBUG;\n    else if (level >= 4)\n        lvl = HC_KAFKA_LOG_INFO;\n    else\n        lvl = HC_KAFKA_LOG_WARN;\n\n    hc_kafka_log(lvl, \"rdkafka: %s\", buf);\n}\n\nstatic void dr_msg_cb(rd_kafka_t *rk,\n                      const rd_kafka_message_t *m,\n                      void *opaque)\n{\n    hc_kafka_client_t *cli = (hc_kafka_client_t *)opaque;\n    hc_kafka_delivery_t d  = {\n        .topic      = m->rkt ? rd_kafka_topic_name(m->rkt) : NULL,\n        .partition hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR,\n                     \"Delivery failed: %s\",\n                     rd_kafka_err2str(m->err));\n    } else {\n        hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_DEBUG,\n                     \"Delivered message to %s [%d] @ %\" PRId64,\n                     d.topic, d.partition, d.offset);\n    }\n    (void)rk;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                              Public  API                                  */\n\nhc_kafka_client_t *hc_kafka_client_new(const hc_kafka_conf_t *user_conf)\n{\n    hc_kafka_client_t *cli = calloc(1, sizeof(*cli));\n    if (!cli) {\n        hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR, \"OOM creating hc_kafka_client\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    cli->conf = rd_kafka_conf_new();\n\n    rd_kafka_conf_set_log_cb(cli->conf, logger_cb);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set_dr_msg_cb(cli->conf, dr_msg_cb);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set_rebalance_cb(cli->conf, rebalance_cb);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set_opaque(cli->conf, cli);\n\n    /* Apply caller-provided overrides */\n    if (user_conf->bootstrap_servers)\n        rd_kafka_conf_set(cli->conf, \"bootstrap.servers\",\n                          user_conf->bootstrap_servers, NULL, 0);\n\n    if (user_conf->client_id)\n        rd_kafka_conf_set(cli->conf, \"client.id\",\n                          user_conf->client_id, NULL, 0);\n\n    if (user_conf->group_id)\n        rd_kafka_conf_set(cli->conf, \"group.id\",\n                          user_conf->group_id, NULL, 0);\n\n    /* Enable idempotent producer for exactly-once semantics */\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(cli->conf, \"enable.idempotence\", \"true\", NULL, 0);\n\n    /* Max in-flight requests set to 5 to prevent re-ordering */\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(cli->conf, \"max.in.flight.requests.per.connection\",\n                      \"5\", NULL, 0);\n\n    char         \"Failed to create Kafka handle: %s\", errstr);\n        rd_kafka_conf_destroy(cli->conf);\n        free(cli);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_init(&cli->producer_mutex, hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_INFO,\n                 \"Kafka client created (id=%s, brokers=%s)\",\n                 cli->client_id, cli->brokers);\n\n    return cli;\n}\n\nvoid hc_kafka_client_destroy(hc_kafka_client_t *cli)\n{\n    if (!cli) return;\n\n    hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_INFO, \"Destroying Kafka client…\");\n\n    cli->run = 0;\n    rd_kafka_flush(cli->rk, 10 * 1000);          /* wait up to 10s */\n\n    rd_kafka_destroy(cli->rk);\n\n    if (cli->subscribed)\n        rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_destroy(cli->subscribed);\n\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&cli->producer_mutex);\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&cli->d_ring.mutex);\n    free(cli->brokers);\n    free(cli->client_id);\n    free(cli);\n}\n\nint hc_kafka_client_subscribe(hc_kafka_client_t *cli,\n                              const char *const *topics,\n                              size_t topic_count)\n{\n    if (!cli || !topics || topic_count == 0)\n        return -EINVAL;\n\n    rd_kafka_conf_t *c_conf = rd_kafka_conf_dup(cli->conf);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(c_conf, \"enable.auto.commit\", \"false\", NULL, 0);\n\n    char errstr[512];\n    rd_kafka_t *c_consumer = rd_kafka_new(RD_KAFKA_CONSUMER, c_conf,\n                                          errstr, sizeof(errstr));\n    if (!c_consumer) {\n        hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR,\n                     \"Failed to create consumer: %s\", errstr);\n        rd_kafka_conf_destroy(c_conf);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    rd_kafka_poll_set_consumer(c_consumer);\n\n    cli->subscribed = rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_new((int)topic_count);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < topic_count; ++i)\n        rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_add(cli->subscribed, topics[i],\n                                          RD_KAFKA_PARTITION_UA);\n\n    rd_kafka_resp_err_t err =\n        rd_kafka_subscribe(c_consumer, cli->subscribed);\n    if (err) {\n        hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR,\n                     \"Subscription failed: %s\",\n                     rd_kafka_err2str(err));\n        rd_kafka_destroy(c_consumer);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Swap the consumer instance into the existing handle */\n    cli->rk = c_consumer;\n    hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_INFO,\n                 \"Subscribed to %zu topic(s)\", topic_count);\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\nint hc_kafka_client_poll(hc_kafka_client_t *cli,\n                         int timeout_ms,\n                         hc_kafka_msg_cb on_msg,\n                         void *opaque)\n{\n    if (!cli || !on_msg || timeout_ms < 0)\n        return -EINVAL;\n\n    rd_kafka_message_t *msg =\n        rd_kafka_consumer_poll(cli->rk, timeout_ms);\n    if (!msg) return 0; /* Timed out */\n\n    if (msg->err) {\n        if (msg->err == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__PARTITION_EOF) {\n            hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_DEBUG,\n                         \"Reached end of %s [%d] @ %\" PRId64,\n                         rd_kafka_topic_name(msg->rkt),\n                         msg->partition, msg->offset);\n        } else {\n            hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR,\n                         \"Consume error: %s\",\n                         rd_kafka_message_errstr(msg));\n        }\n        rd_kafka_message_destroy(msg);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    on_msg(msg, opaque);\n    rd_kafka_message_destroy(msg);\n\n    return 1;\n}\n\nint hc_kafka_client_commit(hc_kafka_client_t *cli)\n{\n    if (!cli) return -EINVAL;\n\n    rd_kafka_resp_err_t err = rd_kafka_commit(cli->rk, NULL, 0);\n    if (err) {\n        hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_WARN,\n                     \"Commit failed: %s\", rd_kafka_err2str(err));\n        return -1;\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n\nint hc_kafka_client_publish(hc_kafka_client_t *cli,\n                            const char *topic,\n                            const char *key,\n                            const void *payload,\n                            size_t len,\n                            int partition,\n                            void *opaque)\n{\n    if (!cli || !topic || !payload)\n        return -EINVAL;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&cli->producer_mutex);\n\n    rd_kafka_resp_err_t err = rd_kafka_producev(\n        cli->rk,\n RD_KAFKA_V_OPAQUE(opaque),\n        RD_KAFKA_V_END);\n\n    if (err) {\n        hc_kafka_log(HC_KAFKA_LOG_ERROR,\n                     \"Produce failed: %s\", rd_kafka_err2str(err));\n cnt;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                           Signal Convenience                              */\n\nstatic volatile int .sa_handler = sig_handler,\n        .sa_flags   = 0\n    };\n    sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);\n    sigaction(SIGINT,  &sa, NULL);\n    sigaction(SIGTERM, &sa, NULL);\n}\n\n/*
